# Final Iranian VPS search continuation — 2026-10-29

Research handoff for [issue #20](https://github.com/Fardinak/torob-aipe-challenge/issues/20), continuing [the additional-source report](vps-additional-search-2026-10-29.md). Read AGENTS.md, docs/agents/domain.md, CONTEXT.md and docs/agents/issue-tracker.md; read issue #20 body and comments through the required repository's GitHub CLI. Applied the research skill's primary-source standard directly; no child agents, as instructed.

**Checkpoint — later admission and final search/family decisions are recorded in [the acquisition review](vps-breadth-review-2026-10-29.md).**

**Search saturation is not established. This report must not be used to close issue #20.** The broad follow-up and directed overlap queries continued to expose distinct providers and resource/package shapes. The useful outcome is a durable, auditable acquisition continuation: all returned fixed cards on the reviewed tariff pages below, including explicit holds and unavailable rows, plus precise remaining gaps. The filename “final” identifies the requested report, not a completion claim. See the stopping assessment near the end.

No catalog, issue, or pre-existing file was edited. No accounts, logins, outreach, reservations, cart additions or purchases were made. Public order URLs are recorded as handoffs, **not visited when they would add a product to a cart**. ITPiran and SeedCloud remain assigned to the parent; the other parent-covered providers named in the task were not needlessly refetched. Earlier successful partial snapshots were reused. Newly followed successful and failed public GETs are retained alongside them. Requests used normal public HTTP access; no challenge or authentication bypass.

## Reading and admission rules

The tables contain **521 source observations**, not 521 unique admitted offers. That count includes unavailable, unpriced, custom, annual-only, currency-held and duplicated landing/store presentations. It also includes 16 unpriced Pouyan IT shapes and 36 WebPouyan OS presentations. Repeated identities across source pages remain evidence variants; they must not inflate offer counts. Counts describe the outcome, never a selection ceiling.

“Reviewable published base” means the returned first-party page identifies a configuration and numeric monthly base price. It is a research admission recommendation, not a checkout quote, stock guarantee, or actual catalog admission. Unless a more precise row or section says otherwise, actual availability, tax basis, mandatory-fee completeness, IPv4 inclusion, licenses, provider backup retention and traffic direction remain **unknown**. An unmarked order button does not prove capacity. Explicit unavailable rows are retained and excluded from current choices. A failed request does not prove withdrawal or advance successful verification.

Use published CPU/core counts, RAM units, disk capacities and storage labels literally. GHz is not a CPU count. MB is not silently converted to GB, and GB is not assumed GiB. SATA and SAS interfaces do not establish SSD/HDD medium. SSD is not silently upgraded to NVMe. Preserve mixed disks, OS/package variants, selected bandwidth and hourly-with-monthly-cap semantics. Annual monthly equivalents are not one-month invoices. Native IRR and USD are retained without invented exchange-rate conversions; exact rial-to-toman division is disclosed only where source units establish it.

Each card table preserves the plan name, resource labels, traffic, tariff, currency/period, stock and other returned card facts in source order, separated by semicolons. This intentionally avoids inventing normalized facts where the provider's fields are ambiguous. Native source tables keep their columns. Public hrefs are copied, never activated. Exclusions override generic base labels; later source-specific reconciliation notes override an earlier missing-field hold when explicitly stated (notably Bia2Host metered landing/store mapping).

Every source section links the original URL, retained response and metadata with retrieval time. All evidence was inspected on **2026-10-29 UTC**. Metadata distinguishes attempted retrieval time from successful-save time; neither is a provider publication date. The final manifest gives SHA-256 of actual retained bytes. Convenience `.txt`, `.links.json`, serialized product extractions and scripts are derived aids; raw responses remain authority.

## Exact cross-provider overlap evidence

These are **resource-exact matches** of advertised CPU count, RAM amount, disk capacity and published storage label on current retained sources. They do not by themselves prove identical hardware package, feature package, OS/license, topology, traffic, included IP, billing variant or region. Unknown mandatory identity fields must remain unknown; this is not authorization to relax catalog grouping rules. Each base tariff belongs to its own provider and terms; never use these as total-cost rankings. Existing first-report snapshots were reused, not re-requested.

Additional nonmatches/holds investigated: 1core/1GB/10GB NVMe NetworkFA landing matches DyarHost ordinary plan1 resources, but NetworkFA handoff404 prevents an equally usable provider choice. Dyar's Respina/Pishgaman pair are **one provider**, not cross-provider duplication. Penta's cheap4GB row is35GB **SSD**, not35GB NVMe. TehranGaming CPU GHz rows cannot establish any CPU-count match. Haio Auto Scale ceilings are not fixed guaranteed RAM. Zagros unknown disk medium cannot establish SSD/NVMe equality. Bia metered SAS Raid is not the unlimited SSD family. Makna /30 is not30 included IPs (directory interpretation was not used). Mixed disks and unknowns were never coerced into approximate matches.

## Search queries and marginal coverage

Searches ran through web search on2026-10-29 UTC. Retained result bundles contain result titles, URLs/excerpts and retrieval output; they are **discovery logs**, not substitutes for first-party response snapshots. Each bundle maps to the exact query batch below. News/secondary marketing comparisons and unrelated software results were ignored for tariff facts. Search result prices often differ from live pages; all admission recommendations use retained current primary tariffs.

Directory/provider-profile fetches were discovery only; provider first-party tariff pages supplied all recommended facts.

## Stopping assessment and next acquisition work

**There is no evidence-based saturation stopping rationale yet.** The final two broad batches were dominated by repeat sources, which is some evidence of diminishing *search-result novelty*, but batch6 still found Abrasim and batch7 found a new Tehran provider with four USD tariffs. Directed followups also added previously unseen OS/storage/CPU-allocation variants, most recently WebPouyan's36 source presentations. “Most results repeated” does not mean the remaining new results are immaterial. Nor do hundreds of observations or dozens of sources satisfy the acceptance criterion by themselves.

This report ends at an evidence-packaging/review handoff boundary, **not because acquisition has achieved the issue's stopping condition**. The breadth/stopping acceptance criterion remains open. No arbitrary number of providers, configurations or queries was imposed or used to label the market complete. It would be misleading to declare that the search stopped adding meaningful choice. A future continuation should use this evidence rather than re-request all successful sources, finish the open linked/public paths, then repeat broad and exact-tuple discovery until the marginal qualifying-provider/resource/package gain actually ceases across assessable rounds.

Concrete unresolved work, in dependency order:

1. Parent reviews/admissions: ingest the reviewable base rows with their billing/traffic/stock/uncertainty; do not count table observations as unique offers. Parent handles ITPiran and SeedCloud and the provider set named in the task. This research did not edit or validate the static catalog, publish data, inspect its UI, or change issue state.
2. Access and identity gaps: Abrasim522/timeout, Berbid403, Xeno500, Saman maintenance, Diako dynamic panel, HiVoo authoritative identity, NetworkFA broken handoff, earlier HighHost/Arianet/Visnet holds. An alternate **public linked** endpoint can resolve a gap; no account, contact or purchase is authorized.
3. Reconcile source conflicts before admitting: Bia unlimited tariff/bandwidth, MT legacy literal-toman values, Didi plan-specific handoff, ServerSetup setup fee, WebPouyan product tariff versus stale JSON-LD/VAT, Makna landing/store mapping, Jahesh disk alternatives, Mizban price period/bandwidth extras, and the first report's resource/location/unit conflicts. Keep valid bases when only total cost is unknown, but never invent required configuration identity facts.
4. Product families outside the explicitly reviewed page bodies remain discovery work: linked Iran GPU/high-storage and other Bia families; ParsVDS HiWeb/location-specific pages; applicable AzarOnline monthly selector and additional Iran OS/storage families; any linked NSup/VPS tariffs not represented by public table8843; other provider families surfaced by navigation. The pages in the tables are exhausted, but this is not an assertion that every product across each provider's whole site has been discovered.
5. The retained secondary directory advertises913 offers/141 providers; those are **the directory's claims**, not reviewed/admitted catalog counts. Its returned page exposed27 provider-profile leads, now cross-referenced to this pass or the first/parent pass, plus a directed RAM-group page which added RTL/SonHost/IranWebHost. The broader directory inventory and any filter/load-more surfaces were not exhausted. Discover first-party URLs for additional names; never import its RAM-only equivalence or prices.
6. Rerun diverse Persian and English broad queries plus exact complete tuples after the queue is worked. Record whether each round adds a new successfully verified eligible provider, a new resource identity, or a material feature/billing variant, separately from inaccessible/index-only leads. Only then assess stopping; do not discard a new provider as immaterial merely because another provider has the same RAM size.
7. Exact overlap findings need parent identity review and coordination with the database/release gate. Resource equality does not resolve unknown feature/hardware/topology fields, and lack of an eligible VPS counterpart never relaxes the managed-database comparison requirement.
